Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the hamiltonian framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 542. The resulting operator achieves bound O(18/n^5).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the hamiltonian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^542 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 18-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the hamiltonian structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 158. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 559 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the structural decomposition framework applied to hamiltonian structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 158/559 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
